Item-level differential detectors require item-level differential ground truth.\n\n\\subsection{DIF as Diagnostic Lens}\n\\label{sec:disc_diagnostic}\n\nThe GSM1K correlation ($r = -0.478$, $p = 0.005$, $N = 33$; \\Cref{sec:gsm8k}) provides exploratory evidence that DIF-flagged items capture behavioral change distinct from memorization. Models with larger contamination gaps benefit \\emph{less} from DIF-C items. Of the 249 DIF-C items on GSM8K, 171 favor the reference (2023) cohort ($\\alpha_{\\mathrm{MH}} > 1$). Partial correlation ($r = -0.385$, $p = 0.027$) after controlling for accuracy confirms the effect is not an artifact of general model quality. Caveats include a wide confidence interval (95\\% CI $= [-0.71, -0.16]$) and limited sample size.\n\nItem characterization reinforces this: DIF-C items are harder ($d = -0.247$) and more discriminating ($d = 0.230$), distributed uniformly across subjects ($\\chi^2$ $p = 0.32$). A difficulty $\\times$ direction interaction ($\\rho = -0.597$, $p < 0.001$) shows capability gains concentrate on hard items (88.7\\% focal-favoring in hardest decile) while easy items erode (96.2\\% reference-favoring in easiest decile).\n\nYen's Q3 analysis confirms that local dependence does not drive DIF: extreme LD correlates negligibly with subject-level C\\% ($r = 0.09$, $p = 0.50$), while reasoning-heavy domains with low LD show higher DIF ($r = -0.36$, $p = 0.006$), consistent with DIF capturing items sensitive to capability evolution.\n\nStructural constraints include: (1) reliable detection requires $N \\geq 80$ per cohort; (2) ability differences confound the DIF signal (within-family C\\% $= 61.3\\%$, reduced to 28.3\\% after ability matching); and (3) MH detects only uniform DIF, making the 31\\% estimate conservative (24.8\\% additional non-uniform DIF at $|\\beta_{\\mathrm{interaction}}| \\geq 0.5$). Key limitations include inability to distinguish contamination from capability change and limited benchmark scope (MMLU + GSM8K).\n\nThese findings motivate a benchmark maintenance protocol: periodic DIF monitoring across model cohorts, item-level stability labels (stable/drifting/unstable) published alongside benchmark releases, and cross-subject matching as the standard protocol for semi-synthetic validation. DIF should be understood as a diagnostic lens, not a quality filter; LLM benchmarks need psychometric monitoring alongside score reporting.\n",

Domain accuracy improvement explains only 2.4 pp of 31.3\\% C\\%; 92.4\\% is item-specific. This does not invalidate MMLU as a ranking instrument ($\\rho \\approx 0.997$) but signals measurement drift across model generations.",

54	Three structural controls fail to reduce temporal C\% below 31\%: matched-ability quintiles, IRT $\theta$-conditioning ($r(\theta, \mathrm{total\ score}) = 0.9986$, FPR $= 0.312$), and within-subject DIF (C\% $= 38.1\%$, exceeding the 25\% bifactor viability threshold). Logistic regression DIF confirms 92.1\% concordance with MH and reveals additional non-uniform DIF (24.8\% at $|\beta_{\mathrm{interaction}}| \geq 0.5$), making the 31\% estimate conservative. Graduated de-duplication ($K{=}10$: 22.7\% vs.\ random 26.8\%; $K{=}20$: 29.1\% vs.\ 30.9\%) confirms family structure does not inflate C\%. Ability-matched within-family analysis reduces Llama-2 vs.\ Llama-3 C\% from 61.3\% to $28.3\% \pm 1.6\%$ (Cohen's $d = -0.99$, overlap $= 0.18$), while temporal matching reduces C\% from 31.3\% to $28.9\% \pm 0.1\%$ ($d = -0.17$, overlap $= 0.76$). The convergence of matched C\% ($\sim$28--29\%) despite vastly different unmatched rates (61.3\% vs.\ 31.3\%) indicates a shared baseline of genuine item-level DIF, with the Llama comparison carrying 33 pp of additional ability confound (54\% reduction) versus only 2.4 pp for temporal cohorts (7.7\% reduction). Full details in \Cref{app:structural}.
55

    "originalFile": "\\section{Experiments}\n\\label{sec:experiments}\n\nWe apply MH-DIF to the METABENCH response matrix~\\citep{kipnis2024metabench} (5,227 models $\\times$ 12,508 MMLU items~\\citep{hendrycks2021mmlu}), with temporal cohorts defined by release date (2023 vs.\\ 2024). Cross-benchmark replication on GSM8K appears in \\Cref{sec:gsm8k}.\n\n\\subsection{Validation: DIF Detection Power}\n\\label{sec:validation}\n\nA power analysis using empirical MMLU IRT parameters confirms that at $N \\geq 80$ per cohort, ETS~C achieves TPR of 0.68--0.80, FPR of 0.036--0.042, and AUC of 0.93--0.94 for items with $|\\Delta_{\\mathrm{MH}}| \\geq 1.4$. Our temporal cohorts ($N_{\\mathrm{ref}} = 1{,}080$, $N_{\\mathrm{focal}} = 807$) far exceed this threshold.\n\n\\subsection{Temporal Comparability: 31\\% Item Instability}\n\\label{sec:temporal}\n\nWe compare 2023 ($N = 1{,}080$) against 2024 ($N = 807$) models; 31.3\\% of items receive ETS~C classification ($|\\Delta_{\\mathrm{MH}}| \\geq 1.5$). Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction at $q = 0.05$ removes only 3 items (31.32\\% $\\to$ 31.30\\%), because the dual ETS threshold on effect size and significance already provides implicit multiple-testing protection.\n\nThree controls confirm this reflects genuine temporal change. A random split of the 2023 cohort yields only 0.18\\% ETS~C items (sampling noise), and an ability-quintile comparison within the same temporal cohort yields 2.9\\% (ability differences alone). A within-family comparison (Llama-2 vs.\\ Llama-3) produces 61.3\\% ETS~C items, elevated by large ability gaps that confound the DIF signal~\\citep{holland1988mh}. The contrast between 2.9\\% (ability-controlled) and 61.3\\% (ability-confounded) brackets the temporal rate of 31.3\\%. Crucially, the temporal cohort ability gap is negligible: Cohen's $d = -0.17$ (below the $|d| = 0.2$ small-effect threshold) with 76.4\\% distributional overlap. Under strict 1:1 nearest-neighbor matching ($d \\approx -0.010$, $N = 638$ per group, 5 seeds), C\\% $= 28.9\\% \\pm 0.1\\%$---a 7.7\\% relative reduction from 31.3\\%, confirming that at most $\\sim$2.4 pp of observed DIF is attributable to ability mismatch.\n\nAcross all 57 MMLU subjects, C\\% ranges from 20.7\\% to 48.4\\% (mean $\\approx 30\\%$); within-subject matching on 5 subjects yields 38.1\\% as a robustness check (\\Cref{tab:temporal_landscape}). Item-level logistic regression shows domain accuracy improvement explains only 2.4 percentage points of 31.3\\% C\\% (pseudo $R^2 = 0.0007$); 92.4\\% of instability is item-specific. The conclusion is robust across thresholds: C\\% $= 48.9\\%$ at $|\\Delta_{\\mathrm{MH}}| \\geq 1.0$, $18.3\\%$ at $\\geq 2.0$, $10.2\\%$ at $\\geq 2.5$.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\caption{DIF landscape across cohort definitions. ETS~C denotes the percentage of items with $|\\Delta_{\\mathrm{MH}}| \\geq 1.5$. FPR is measured against Li et al.\\ web-overlap labels on the ``clean'' (non-overlapping) subset. Enrichment is the ratio of observed to expected overlap between DIF flags and web-overlap labels.}\n\\label{tab:temporal_landscape}\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{lcccc}\n\\toprule\nCohort Definition & $N_{\\mathrm{ref}}$ / $N_{\\mathrm{focal}}$ & C\\% & FPR (clean) & Enrich. \\\\\n\\midrule\nTemporal (2023 vs.\\ 2024) & 1,080 / 807 & 31.3 & 0.318 & 0.96 \\\\\nAbility quintile (Q1--2 vs.\\ Q4--5) & ${\\sim}$2,600 / ${\\sim}$2,600 & 2.9 & 0.027 & 1.19 \\\\\nWithin-family (Llama-2 vs.\\ 3) & 161 / 308 & 61.3 & -- & -- \\\\\nRandom split (sanity) & 540 / 540 & 0.18 & -- & -- \\\\\nWithin-subject (avg, 5 subj.) & \\textit{varies} & 38.1 & -- & -- \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\subsection{Ground Truth Mismatch}\n\\label{sec:groundtruth}\n\nDirect comparison of DIF flags against the web-overlap labels of \\citet{li2024contamination} on 9,229 annotated items confirms that the two methods capture orthogonal signals. The enrichment ratio is 0.964 (\\Cref{tab:temporal_landscape}): DIF-C rates are 30.7\\% among web-contaminated items and 31.8\\% among clean items. Association tests yield $\\phi = 0.012$, $\\chi^2 = 1.28$ ($p = 0.258$), and OR $= 1.05$ [0.96, 1.15]; the Jaccard index between flagged sets is 0.206. This near-independence holds across contamination subtypes (input-only enrichment $= 0.986$, input-and-label $= 0.960$). The null result does not indicate detection failure: semi-synthetic injection produces a clear dose-response ($\\Delta\\mathrm{TPR} = {+}0.508$ at $p = 0.5$; \\Cref{fig:dose_response}). The disconnect arises because web-overlap labels measure \\emph{global exposure} (whether an item exists in training corpora), while DIF measures \\emph{differential functioning} (whether an item's relationship to ability changes across cohorts). The Li et al.\\ labels are the wrong ground truth for a differential method.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig_2_dose_response.pdf}\n\\caption{Semi-synthetic dose-response for DIF detection on MMLU. $\\Delta\\mathrm{TPR}$ increases monotonically with exploitation rate $p$, confirming that MH-DIF detects differential contamination when present. Baseline (no injection) produces $\\Delta\\mathrm{TPR} \\approx 0$.}\n\\label{fig:dose_response}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\subsection{Structural Analysis}\n\\label{sec:structural}\n\nThree structural controls fail to reduce temporal C\\% below 31\\%: matched-ability quintiles, IRT $\\theta$-conditioning ($r(\\theta, \\mathrm{total\\ score}) = 0.9986$, FPR $= 0.312$), and within-subject DIF (C\\% $= 38.1\\%$, exceeding the 25\\% bifactor viability threshold). Logistic regression DIF confirms 92.1\\% concordance with MH and reveals additional non-uniform DIF (24.8\\% at $|\\beta_{\\mathrm{interaction}}| \\geq 0.5$), making the 31\\% estimate conservative. Graduated de-duplication ($K{=}10$: 22.7\\% vs.\\ random 26.8\\%; $K{=}20$: 29.1\\% vs.\\ 30.9\\%) confirms family structure does not inflate C\\%. Ability-matched within-family analysis reduces Llama-2 vs.\\ Llama-3 C\\% from 61.3\\% to $28.3\\% \\pm 1.6\\%$ (Cohen's $d = -0.99$, overlap $= 0.18$), while temporal matching reduces C\\% from 31.3\\% to $28.9\\% \\pm 0.1\\%$ ($d = -0.17$, overlap $= 0.76$). The convergence of matched C\\% ($\\sim$28--29\\%) despite vastly different unmatched rates (61.3\\% vs.\\ 31.3\\%) indicates a shared baseline of genuine item-level DIF, with the Llama comparison carrying 33 pp of additional ability confound (54\\% reduction) versus only 2.4 pp for temporal cohorts (7.7\\% reduction). Full details in \\Cref{app:structural}.\n\n\\subsection{Semi-Synthetic Framework Validation}\n\\label{sec:semisynthetic}\n\nThe critical methodological finding is that the choice of matching strategy determines validity. Cross-subject matching computes the conditioning variable from subjects \\emph{other than} the one being tested, achieving $\\Delta\\mathrm{FPR} = 0.000$ across all five MMLU subjects while four of five produce $\\Delta\\mathrm{TPR} > 0.3$ at $p = 0.5$. Within-subject matching inflates FPR by ${+}0.186$ on average because the matching variable absorbs part of the injection signal. We adopt cross-subject matching as the standard protocol (\\Cref{app:fig_matching}).\n\n\\subsection{Downstream Impact}\n\\label{sec:downstream}\n\nSpearman correlation between full and stable-item rankings is $\\rho \\approx 0.997$: aggregate model ordering is preserved despite 31\\% item instability. DIF-flagged items nonetheless carry more discriminative signal than average ($z = -8$ to $-49$ vs.\\ random removal across 21 conditions), though a circularity ablation shows DIF-based removal is the \\emph{least} disruptive among non-random strategies (Jaccard overlap with high-discrimination items is only 0.12). Individual model families show distinctive DIF profiles: Llama-3 derivatives gain a median of ${+}53$ rank positions (mean ${+}121$), while Mistral-based models lose a median of $35$ (mean $-54$); individual extremes reach ${+}793$ and $-542$ (\\Cref{app:fig_downstream}). Overall, 25\\% of models shift by $|\\Delta\\mathrm{Rank}| \\geq 100$. The DIF instability pattern extends beyond MMLU; \\Cref{sec:gsm8k} presents a full replication on GSM8K.\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Benchmark Validation: GSM8K}\n\\label{sec:gsm8k}\n\nReplication on GSM8K (1,319 items $\\times$ 6,702 models) yields temporal C\\% $= 18.9\\%$ (sanity check: 0.3\\%), with semi-synthetic $\\Delta\\mathrm{TPR} = {+}0.514$ at $p = 0.5$ under external matching (\\Cref{app:gsm8k_details}). As an exploratory analysis, the GSM1K held-out set~\\citep{zhang2024gsm1k} provides independent contamination validation: for $N = 33$ models, the correlation between contamination gap and DIF ranking advantage is $r = -0.478$ ($p = 0.005$; partial $r = -0.385$, $p = 0.027$ after controlling for accuracy). This \\emph{negative} correlation contradicts the hypothesis that DIF items benefit contaminated models, and instead suggests that DIF captures genuine capability change distinct from memorization (\\Cref{fig:gsm1k}). The result is suggestive at $N = 33$ and warrants replication.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig_5_gsm1k_dif_correlation.pdf}\n\\caption{GSM1K contamination gap vs.\\ DIF ranking advantage ($N = 33$). The negative correlation ($r = -0.478$, $p = 0.005$) indicates that models with larger contamination gaps benefit less from DIF-flagged items.}\n\\label{fig:gsm1k}\n\\end{figure}\n",

      {

        "oldLines": 7,
        "newStart": 51,
        "newLines": 7,
        "lines": [
          " \\subsection{Structural Analysis}",
          " \\label{sec:structural}",
          " ",
          "-Three structural controls fail to reduce temporal C\\% below 31\\%: matched-ability quintiles, IRT $\\theta$-conditioning ($r(\\theta, \\mathrm{total\\ score}) = 0.9986$, FPR $= 0.312$), and within-subject DIF (C\\% $= 38.1\\%$, exceeding the 25\\% bifactor viability threshold). Logistic regression DIF confirms 92.1\\% concordance with MH and reveals additional non-uniform DIF (24.8\\% at $|\\beta_{\\mathrm{interaction}}| \\geq 0.5$), making the 31\\% estimate conservative. Graduated de-duplication ($K{=}10$: 22.7\\% vs.\\ random 26.8\\%; $K{=}20$: 29.1\\% vs.\\ 30.9\\%) confirms family structure does not inflate C\\%. Ability-matched within-family analysis reduces Llama-2 vs.\\ Llama-3 C\\% from 61.3\\% to $28.3\\% \\pm 1.6\\%$ (Cohen's $d = -0.99$, overlap $= 0.18$), while temporal matching reduces C\\% from 31.3\\% to $28.9\\% \\pm 0.1\\%$ ($d = -0.17$, overlap $= 0.76$). The convergence of matched C\\% ($\\sim$28--29\\%) despite vastly different unmatched rates (61.3\\% vs.\\ 31.3\\%) indicates a shared baseline of genuine item-level DIF, with the Llama comparison carrying 33 pp of additional ability confound (54\\% reduction) versus only 2.4 pp for temporal cohorts (7.7\\% reduction). Full details in \\Cref{app:structural}.",

Logistic regression DIF confirms 92.1\\% concordance with MH and reveals additional non-uniform DIF (24.8\\% at $|\\beta_{\\mathrm{interaction}}| \\geq 0.5$), making the 31\\% estimate conservative. Graduated de-duplication ($K{=}10$: 22.7\\% vs.\\ random 26.8\\%; $K{=}20$: 29.1\\% vs.\\ 30.9\\%) confirms family structure does not inflate C\\%. Ability-matched within-family analysis reduces Llama-2 vs.\\ Llama-3 C\\% from 61.3\\% to $28.3\\% \\pm 1.6\\%$ (Cohen's $d = -0.99$, overlap $= 0.18$), while temporal matching reduces C\\% from 31.3\\% to $28.9\\% \\pm 0.1\\%$ ($d = -0.17$, overlap $= 0.76$). The matched C\\% values ($\\sim$28--29\\%) are consistent with each other despite vastly different unmatched rates (61.3\\% vs.\\ 31.3\\%), suggesting that genuine item-level DIF accounts for $\\sim$28--29\\% of items, with the Llama comparison carrying 33 pp of additional ability confound (54\\% reduction) versus only 2.4 pp for temporal cohorts (7.7\\% reduction). <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_018krVwRywHF1Z8MxWJMEy4g`</summary>

```text
42	Direct comparison of DIF flags against the web-overlap labels of \citet{li2024contamination} on 9,229 annotated items confirms that the two methods capture orthogonal signals. The enrichment ratio is 0.964 (\Cref{tab:temporal_landscape}): DIF-C rates are 30.7\% among web-contaminated items and 31.8\% among clean items. Association tests yield $\phi = 0.012$, $\chi^2 = 1.28$ ($p = 0.258$), and OR $= 1.05$ [0.96, 1.15]; the Jaccard index between flagged sets is 0.206. This near-independence holds across contamination subtypes (input-only enrichment $= 0.986$, input-and-label $= 0.960$). The null result does not indicate detection failure: semi-synthetic injection produces a clear dose-response ($\Delta\mathrm{TPR} = {+}0.508$ at $p = 0.5$; \Cref{fig:dose_response}). The disconnect arises because web-overlap labels measure \emph{global exposure} (whether an item exists in training corpora), while DIF measures \emph{differential functioning} (whether an item's relationship to ability changes across cohorts). The Li et al.\ labels are the wrong ground truth for a differential method.
43	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_016d9EgYNVTJofXX4ZR2Je8s`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: paper-refinement
3	description: Step 5 论文迭代修改循环——Reviewer 评分循环 + 收敛判断 + paper_checklist 注入，max 5 轮。Paper Agent 进入 Step 5 时 Read 本 skill。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	  - workflow
7	  - research
8	  - writing
9	  - academic
10	  - review
11	version: 1
12	roles:
13	  - paper_agent
14	---
15	
16	# Step 5 — Iterative Refinement
17	
18	Step 4 全部章节落盘后，Paper Agent 进入 Step 5：把全文递交 Reviewer 评分，按反馈修改，直到收敛。
19	
20	**核心约束**：
21	- 串行——一轮 Reviewer 返回结果处理完才进下一轮，禁止并发请求
22	- 评分驱动——overall 提升才 accept 本轮，下降则 git revert
23	- 上限 5 轮——硬约束，达到上限即停，不论分数
24	
25	## 状态记录（git 是 truth source）
26	
27	每轮修改前 commit 当前论文目录，作为 revert 锚点。无需额外 state 文件。
28	
29	```
30	git -C projects/{project} add paper/
31	git -C projects/{project} commit -m "[paper] refinement round {N-1}"
32	```
33	
34	Round N 的 commit message 用 `N-1`，即 round 1 修改前 commit `round 0`（Step 4 落盘后的初稿）。
35	
36	## 单轮流程
37	
38	```
39	Round N（N = 1..5）：
40	
41	1. git commit 当前版本（message: "[paper] refinement round N-1"）
42	   └── 落地评分下降时的 revert 锚点
43	
44	2. 组装审查请求：
45	   - read_project_file("paper_checklist.yaml") — 用户自定义必查项
46	   - 拼 LaTeX 全文（或 Patch 模式下的差异）+ writing_plan.yaml 路径
47	   - 末尾追加 "## 用户自定义必查项"（见下方"checklist 注入格式"）
48	
49	3. paper_agent_request_review(text=组装后的请求, project=...)
50	   └── 请求末尾追加维度提醒："除标准的 Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation 维度外，请额外检查：叙事一致性（各章节是否讲同一个故事）、贡献-范围对齐（Intro contribution ↔ 正文 section 1:1）、去 AI 风格（em-dash、-ing、filler words）。参考 reviewer-audit-checklist §7。"
51	   └── Reviewer 通过 reviewer_post_to_director 回传评分
52	
53	4. 解析 Reviewer 响应：
54	   - 维度分（Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation，1-4）
55	   - overall（1-10）
56	   - 各条具体问题 severity（critical/warning/info）
57	   - checklist 项 PASS/FAIL
58	   ★ 任一 checklist FAIL → 该条问题 severity 自动按 critical 对待
59	
60	5. 按问题类型分流处理：
````

      "content": "---\nname: paper-refinement\ndescription: Step 5 论文迭代修改循环——Reviewer 评分循环 + 收敛判断 + paper_checklist 注入，max 5 轮。Paper Agent 进入 Step 5 时 Read 本 skill。\nscope: global\ntags:\n  - workflow\n  - research\n  - writing\n  - academic\n  - review\nversion: 1\nroles:\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# Step 5 — Iterative Refinement\n\nStep 4 全部章节落盘后，Paper Agent 进入 Step 5：把全文递交 Reviewer 评分，按反馈修改，直到收敛。\n\n**核心约束**：\n- 串行——一轮 Reviewer 返回结果处理完才进下一轮，禁止并发请求\n- 评分驱动——overall 提升才 accept 本轮，下降则 git revert\n- 上限 5 轮——硬约束，达到上限即停，不论分数\n\n## 状态记录（git 是 truth source）\n\n每轮修改前 commit 当前论文目录，作为 revert 锚点。无需额外 state 文件。\n\n```\ngit -C projects/{project} add paper/\ngit -C projects/{project} commit -m \"[paper] refinement round {N-1}\"\n```\n\nRound N 的 commit message 用 `N-1`，即 round 1 修改前 commit `round 0`（Step 4 落盘后的初稿）。\n\n## 单轮流程\n\n```\nRound N（N = 1..5）：\n\n1. git commit 当前版本（message: \"[paper] refinement round N-1\"）\n   └── 落地评分下降时的 revert 锚点\n\n2. 组装审查请求：\n   - read_project_file(\"paper_checklist.yaml\") — 用户自定义必查项\n   - 拼 LaTeX 全文（或 Patch 模式下的差异）+ writing_plan.yaml 路径\n   - 末尾追加 \"## 用户自定义必查项\"（见下方\"checklist 注入格式\"）\n\n3. paper_agent_request_review(text=组装后的请求, project=...)\n   └── 请求末尾追加维度提醒：\"除标准的 Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation 维度外，请额外检查：叙事一致性（各章节是否讲同一个故事）、贡献-范围对齐（Intro contribution ↔ 正文 section 1:1）、去 AI 风格（em-dash、-ing、filler words）。参考 reviewer-audit-checklist §7。\"\n   └── Reviewer 通过 reviewer_post_to_director 回传评分\n\n4. 解析 Reviewer 响应：\n   - 维度分（Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation，1-4）\n   - overall（1-10）\n   - 各条具体问题 severity（critical/warning/info）\n   - checklist 项 PASS/FAIL\n   ★ 任一 checklist FAIL → 该条问题 severity 自动按 critical 对待\n\n5. 按问题类型分流处理：",
      "numLines": 60,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 241
    }
  },

60	5. 按问题类型分流处理：
61	   - 事实性错误（数字不一致 / 引用错位）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section 改
62	   - 段落级叙事问题（逻辑断裂 / 强度词超限）→ spawn Worker 重写段落，回来再 edit_paper_section
63	   - 结构问题（章节顺序 / 段落归并）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section
64	   - critical 且 claim 本身无支撑 → 汇报 Director（不要自行降 claim）
65	
66	6. 全部问题处理完后判断本轮 overall 走势：
67	   - overall(N) ≥ overall(N-1) → 保留本轮修改，进入收敛判断
68	   - overall(N) <  overall(N-1) → git checkout 上一轮 commit，回退本轮所有修改，标记"本轮无进展"
69	
70	7. 收敛判断（任一满足即停）：
71	   - 全部问题为 info（无 critical / warning）
72	   - N == 5（硬上限）
73	   - 连续两轮 overall 持平（差 ≤0.1）
74	   - 连续两轮无新增 critical（critical 列表内容相同或更短）
75	
76	   未收敛且 N<5 → 进入 Round N+1
77	   已收敛 → 进入收尾（见下文）
78	```
79	
80	## checklist 注入格式
81	
82	`read_project_file("paper_checklist.yaml")` 拿到 items 后，在审查请求末尾追加：
83	
84	```
85	## 用户自定义必查项
86	以下是用户指定的必须检查的项，每项必须明确标注 PASS 或 FAIL：
87	- [chk_001] {text}
88	- [chk_002] {text}
89	...
90	
91	如果任何一项标注为 FAIL，整体 severity 自动提升为 critical（必须打回修改）。
92	```
93	
94	- checklist 为空 / 文件不存在 → 不追加该段，正常请求
95	- 不要重写 checklist 文本——保持用户原话，让 Reviewer 自己定位
96	- Dream 模式禁止注入（Dream 论文不投，格式检查无意义）
97	
98	## 修改 Worker 任务模板
99	
100	段落级修改派 Worker（不是整章重写）时：
101	
102	```
103	spawn_worker(
104	  task_type="paper_writing",

108	
109	【问题位置】{paragraph_id / 行号}
110	【问题描述】{Reviewer 原文}
111	【建议方向】{Reviewer 给的方向}
112	
113	当前段落原文：
114	```latex
115	{原文片段}
116	```
117	
118	参考资料：
119	- 写作规范：Read skills/global/latex-paper.skill.md + red-section.skill.md
120	- registry 切片：{相关 exp_id 列表}
121	- 同章节已有内容：{section}.tex 整章
122	- 引用：references.bib（只用已 verified 条目，未验证标 [UNVERIFIED]）
123	
124	要求：
125	- 只改问题段落，不动其他内容
126	- 输出修改后的段落 latex（标记 old / new）
127	- 不要画图、不要补实验
128	- 改完即返回，不写额外说明
129	"""
130	)
131	```

133	Worker 返回后 Paper Agent 用 `edit_paper_section(project, section, old_text=..., new_text=..., claims=..., table_cells=..., figures=...)` 落盘——`edit_paper_section` 自动同步 provenance，不会 deprecate 未触及的 claim。

136	
137	Reviewer 评分循环收敛后**必须执行完整三件套**——视觉 + 引用 + 溯源**全部跑完**再汇报 Director；不允许只跑一两项就提交。任一发现 critical/blocker 都要在汇报中显式列出，不得"建议提交审稿"压过失败信号。
138

````text
139	### 三件套执行顺序
140	
141	按下面顺序串行（每一步完成才进下一步，前一步失败不绕过）：
142	
143	```
144	1. paper-visual-review 排版检查
145	   └── Read skills/global/paper-visual-review.skill.md
146	   └── pdf_to_images(pdf_path=projects/{project}/paper/main.pdf, pages="1-N", dpi=200)
147	       逐页检查图表分辨率 / 字号 / 边距溢出 / 公式 / 引用编号 / 缺失引用占位 [?]
148	   └── cleanup_pdf_images(output_dir=<上一步返回的 output_dir>)  # 不留临时文件
149	   └── 收集 findings：[{page, severity, issue}]
150	   └── 出现 critical（图表溢出 / 缺失编号 / 公式乱码）→ 记入 visual_report，**不**立即返工，
151	       与 verify_references / check_provenance_completeness 的结果**一并汇报 Director**
152	
153	2. verify_references 最终引用验证
154	   └── verify_references(paper_dir="projects/{project}/paper", project=..., skip_online=False)
155	   └── 返回字段：status (ok/orphan_bib/missing_bib/not_found/mismatch) 按条聚合
156	   └── critical 阈值：
157	        - missing_bib（正文 \cite 但 .bib 缺）→ 必须修，否则 LaTeX 编译失败
158	        - not_found（OpenAlex 找不到）→ 疑似幻觉引用，自己 WebSearch 二次验证；仍找不到 → critical
159	        - mismatch（年份/作者偏差）→ warning
160	   └── 自己能改的小问题（missing_bib、年份） → edit_paper_section 修；改完**再调一次 verify_references** 确认通过
161	
162	3. check_provenance_completeness 溯源覆盖
163	   └── check_provenance_completeness(project=...)
164	   └── 返回：tier_distribution / untracked_numbers / invalidated_claims
165	   └── 关键阈值：
166	        - Tier 3 占比 > 30% → 必须汇报 Director（红线，不得静默交付）
167	        - Tier X（invalidated，实验失败/被作废）≥ 1 条 → critical：claim 失去支撑，汇报 Director 决策是否改 claim
168	        - untracked_numbers（正文出现但 provenance 没追踪的数字）≥ 5 → warning：派 Worker 补 provenance
169	```
170	
171	### 汇报 Director 模板
172	
173	三件套全部跑完后**统一**汇报（不要分三次发），把所有 critical/warning 列在一处便于 Director 决策：
174	
175	```
176	paper_agent_post_to_director(
177	  text="[写作进展] Step 5 收尾完成
178	         迭代轮数：{N}/5
179	         最终 overall：{score}
180	         checklist：全部 PASS / 还有 X 条 FAIL（已修复 / 待 Director 决策）
181	
182	         ===== 视觉检查 =====
183	         检查页数：{n}/{total}
184	         结论：PASS / 发现 X 条 critical + Y 条 warning
185	         {若有 critical：逐条列出 page + 描述}
186	
187	         ===== 引用验证 =====
188	         总数 X 条，已验证 Y 条
189	         critical：missing_bib X 条 + 疑似幻觉 Y 条（已尝试 WebSearch 二次验证 → 结果）
190	         warning：mismatch X 条
191	         {若有 critical：列出条目 + 已采取的修复}
192	
193	         ===== Provenance 覆盖 =====
194	         Tier 分布：T1 {a}% / T2 {b}% / T3 {c}% / TX {d}%
195	         {若 T3 > 30% 或 TX ≥ 1：列出受影响的 claim/figure/table}
196	         未追踪数字：X 个（详见 untracked_numbers）
197	
198	         ===== 建议 =====
199	         {以下任选其一}
200	         A. 视觉/引用/provenance 全部 PASS → 建议提交审稿（submit_paper_to_review）
201	         B. 存在 critical → 请 Director 决策：{具体问题列表 + 建议修复方向}
202	         C. provenance 红线触发 → 请 Director 决定：补实验 / 改 claim / 接受现状",
203	  project=...
204	)
205	```
206	
207	Director 决定是否调 `submit_paper_to_review`——Paper Agent **不**主动调，这是 Director 的权限。
208	
209	### 红线（Step 5e 不可绕过）
210	
211	1. **三件套必须全跑**：跳过任一项直接汇报 = 违规，被 Director 打回时不接受"我以为不必要"
212	2. **critical 不静默**：视觉 critical / verify_references critical / Tier X ≥ 1 / Tier 3 > 30% 必须显式写进汇报
213	3. **不主动提交审稿**：哪怕全 PASS 也只能"建议提交"，submit_paper_to_review 是 Director 权限
214	4. **修复后必须复验**：自己修了 missing_bib → 重跑 verify_references；补 provenance → 重跑 check_provenance_completeness
215	5. **Patch 模式走 Phase E**：Patch 的收尾在 Phase E（max 3 轮 Reviewer），不复用 Step 5e 全套；Phase E 自己做 check_provenance_completeness + verify_references，视觉检查可选
216	
217	## 硬约束（与 Paper Agent 红线一致）
218	
````
